# Task
You will be given a **Requirement** and a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML. Your task is to annotate every operation listed in **Operations** with a specification.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Take the principle of modularization into account.
2. For each operation, determine its functional boundary based on the given requirement. Ensure high cohesion and low coupling.
3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.
4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
Airlines offer different flights. Each flight has its unique id. A flight is open for booking and closed by order of the company. A customer can book one or more flights, for different passengers. A reservation concerns a single flight and a single passenger, has a unique id. A reservation can be confirmed or canceled. A flight has a departure airport and an arrival airport. Each airport has its unique id. A flight has a day and time of departure, and a day and time of arrival (e.g. 2024-12-08 13:00:00). A flight may include stopovers in one or more airport(s). A stopover has a departure time and an arrival time. Each airport serves one or more cities.
Functional requirement:
1.Publish a flight for booking. An airline can publish a newly created flight to make it available for customer bookings. The system first confirms that the flight has valid departure and arrival timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, then checks temporal consistency — currentTime < departureTime < arrivalTime — and route integrity — departureAirport ≠ arrivalAirport. A flight may be published only once, and before publishing, the flight's openForBooking status must be false. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
2.Create a booking for passengers on a flight. A customer selects a specific open flight and supplies a list of passengers names. The system checks that there are no duplicate passengers on the flight and the current time is before the flight departure time. It then generates a booking for the customer and creates a reservation with a unique ID for each passenger in that booking. Each reservation is initially placed in "pending" status until the customer confirms it. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
3.Confirm or cancel an existing reservation in a booking. The system checks that the flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking. The customer can then confirm or cancel a reservation by providing its reservation ID. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
4.Close an open flight. An airline can close an existing flight that has not yet departed. The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
5.Retrieve all confirmed reservations for a specific flight. Given a flight object, the system retrieves every confirmed reservation on that flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking. It returns a list of reservations, or empty list if none exist.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title flights Model

class Airline {
    flights : List<Flight>
    addFlight(f : Flight) : void
    closeFlight(flightId : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    publishFlight(f : Flight, now : EDate) : boolean
    removeFlight(f : Flight) : void
    searchFlights(origin : String, dest : String, date : EDate) : List<Flight>
}
class Airport {
    id : String
    servesForCities : List<City>
    addCity(c : City) : void
}
class Booking {
    customer : Customer
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    createReservation(f : Flight, passenger : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class City {
}
class Customer {
    bookings : List<Booking>
    addBooking(f : Flight, now : EDate, listOfPassengerNames : List<String>) : boolean
    cancel(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    confirm(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class Flight {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    id : String
    openForBooking : boolean
    arrivalAirport : Airport
    departureAirport : Airport
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    stopovers : List<Stopover>
    addStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
    getConfirmedReservations() : List<Reservation>
    removeStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class Passenger {
    name : String
}
class Reservation {
    id : String
    status : ReservationStatus
    flight : Flight
    passenger : Passenger
}
class Stopover {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    airport : Airport
}
enum ReservationStatus {
    PENDING
    CONFIRMED
    CANCELED
}

Airline *-- "*" Flight : flights
Airport --> "*" City : servesForCities
Booking --> Customer : customer
Booking *-- "*" Reservation : reservations
Customer --> "*" Booking : bookings
Flight --> Airport : arrivalAirport
Flight --> Airport : departureAirport
Flight --> "*" Reservation : reservations  'opposite: flight
Flight *-- "*" Stopover : stopovers
Reservation --> Flight : flight  'opposite: reservations
Reservation --> Passenger : passenger
Stopover --> Airport : airport
@enduml
```

# Task
You will be given a **Requirement**, a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML, and a list of operation specifications. Your task is to correct the specifications that are inconsistent with the requirement.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Read the requirement and the class diagram.
2. For each operation specification, determine whether it is complete and consistent with the requirement.
3. If there is any error, correct the erroneous specification.
4. Follow the specification format. Output changed specifications only; OMIT unchanged specifications.

# Requirement
```text
Airlines offer different flights. Each flight has its unique id. A flight is open for booking and closed by order of the company. A customer can book one or more flights, for different passengers. A reservation concerns a single flight and a single passenger, has a unique id. A reservation can be confirmed or canceled. A flight has a departure airport and an arrival airport. Each airport has its unique id. A flight has a day and time of departure, and a day and time of arrival (e.g. 2024-12-08 13:00:00). A flight may include stopovers in one or more airport(s). A stopover has a departure time and an arrival time. Each airport serves one or more cities.
Functional requirement:
1.Publish a flight for booking. An airline can publish a newly created flight to make it available for customer bookings. The system first confirms that the flight has valid departure and arrival timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, then checks temporal consistency — currentTime < departureTime < arrivalTime — and route integrity — departureAirport ≠ arrivalAirport. A flight may be published only once, and before publishing, the flight's openForBooking status must be false. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
2.Create a booking for passengers on a flight. A customer selects a specific open flight and supplies a list of passengers names. The system checks that there are no duplicate passengers on the flight and the current time is before the flight departure time. It then generates a booking for the customer and creates a reservation with a unique ID for each passenger in that booking. Each reservation is initially placed in "pending" status until the customer confirms it. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
3.Confirm or cancel an existing reservation in a booking. The system checks that the flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking. The customer can then confirm or cancel a reservation by providing its reservation ID. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
4.Close an open flight. An airline can close an existing flight that has not yet departed. The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
5.Retrieve all confirmed reservations for a specific flight. Given a flight object, the system retrieves every confirmed reservation on that flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking. It returns a list of reservations, or empty list if none exist.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title flights Model

class Airline {
    flights : List<Flight>
    addFlight(f : Flight) : void
    closeFlight(flightId : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    publishFlight(f : Flight, now : EDate) : boolean
    removeFlight(f : Flight) : void
    searchFlights(origin : String, dest : String, date : EDate) : List<Flight>
}
class Airport {
    id : String
    servesForCities : List<City>
    addCity(c : City) : void
}
class Booking {
    customer : Customer
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    createReservation(f : Flight, passenger : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class City {
}
class Customer {
    bookings : List<Booking>
    addBooking(f : Flight, now : EDate, listOfPassengerNames : List<String>) : boolean
    cancel(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    confirm(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class Flight {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    id : String
    openForBooking : boolean
    arrivalAirport : Airport
    departureAirport : Airport
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    stopovers : List<Stopover>
    addStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
    getConfirmedReservations() : List<Reservation>
    removeStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class Passenger {
    name : String
}
class Reservation {
    id : String
    status : ReservationStatus
    flight : Flight
    passenger : Passenger
}
class Stopover {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    airport : Airport
}
enum ReservationStatus {
    PENDING
    CONFIRMED
    CANCELED
}

Airline *-- "*" Flight : flights
Airport --> "*" City : servesForCities
Booking --> Customer : customer
Booking *-- "*" Reservation : reservations
Customer --> "*" Booking : bookings
Flight --> Airport : arrivalAirport
Flight --> Airport : departureAirport
Flight --> "*" Reservation : reservations  'opposite: flight
Flight *-- "*" Stopover : stopovers
Reservation --> Flight : flight  'opposite: reservations
Reservation --> Passenger : passenger
Stopover --> Airport : airport
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
Customer::addBooking
Summary: Creates a new Booking for this customer on a chosen flight, with one PENDING Reservation per supplied passenger name.
Algorithm:
  1. If f is null, listOfPassengerNames is null/empty, or now is null, return false.
  2. If f.openForBooking == false, return false.
  3. If now is not before f.departureTime, return false.
  4. If listOfPassengerNames contains duplicate names (case-insensitive trimmed compare), return false.
  5. Create a new Booking with customer = this.
  6. For each passengerName in listOfPassengerNames:
       - Call newBooking.createReservation(f, passengerName, now); if it returns false, abort and return false.
  7. Append newBooking to this customer's bookings list.
  8. Return true.
Related Features: bookings (List<Booking>)
Related Classes: Flight, Booking, Reservation, Passenger, ReservationStatus
Input:
  - f : Flight — the open flight the customer is booking.
  - now : EDate — current date/time.
  - listOfPassengerNames : List<String> — non-empty list of distinct passenger names.
Output: boolean — true if the booking (and all its reservations) is created, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: f is open for booking, now < f.departureTime, listOfPassengerNames is non-empty and contains no duplicates.
Post-condition: A new Booking is added to this customer's bookings list; one PENDING Reservation per passenger name exists on flight f and is linked to the new booking.

Customer::cancel
Summary: Cancels an existing reservation owned by this customer, provided the flight is still open and has not departed.
Algorithm:
  1. If reservationID is null/empty or now is null, return false.
  2. Search this customer's bookings for a Booking containing a Reservation whose id equals reservationID; if none, return false.
  3. Let r be the matching reservation; let f be r.flight.
  4. If f.openForBooking == false, return false.
  5. If now is not before f.departureTime, return false.
  6. Set r.status = CANCELED.
  7. Return true.
Related Features: bookings (List<Booking>)
Related Classes: Booking, Reservation, Flight, ReservationStatus
Input:
  - reservationID : String — unique id of the reservation to cancel.
  - now : EDate — current date/time.
Output: boolean — true on successful cancellation, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: A reservation with the given id exists within one of this customer's bookings; its flight is still open for booking; current time is before the flight's departureTime; reservation status is not already CANCELED.
Post-condition: The reservation's status is CANCELED; the flight's other reservations remain unchanged.

Customer::confirm
Summary: Confirms an existing reservation owned by this customer, provided the flight is still open and has not departed.
Algorithm:
  1. If reservationID is null/empty or now is null, return false.
  2. Search this customer's bookings for a Booking containing a Reservation whose id equals reservationID; if none, return false.
  3. Let r be the matching reservation; let f be r.flight.
  4. If f.openForBooking == false, return false.
  5. If now is not before f.departureTime, return false.
  6. If r.status is already CONFIRMED, return true (idempotent).
  7. If r.status == CANCELED, return false (cannot confirm a canceled reservation).
  8. Set r.status = CONFIRMED.
  9. Return true.
Related Features: bookings (List<Booking>)
Related Classes: Booking, Reservation, Flight, ReservationStatus
Input:
  - reservationID : String — unique id of the reservation to confirm.
  - now : EDate — current date/time.
Output: boolean — true on successful confirmation, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: A reservation with the given id exists within one of this customer's bookings; its flight is still open for booking; current time is before the flight's departureTime; the reservation is currently PENDING.
Post-condition: The reservation's status becomes CONFIRMED; no other reservation is affected.

The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
```text
5.Retrieve all confirmed reservations for a specific flight. Given a flight object, the system retrieves every confirmed reservation on that flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking. It returns a list of reservations, or empty list if none exist.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title flights Model

class Airline {
    flights : List<Flight>
    addFlight(f : Flight) : void
    closeFlight(flightId : String, now : EDate) : boolean

    reservations : List<Reservation>

}

}

    bookings : List<Booking>

    cancel(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    confirm(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean

    arrivalTime : EDate

    PENDING
    CONFIRMED

Booking *-- "*" Reservation : reservations
Customer --> "*" Booking : bookings
Flight --> Airport : arrivalAirport
Flight --> Airport : departureAirport
```

# Operation Specifications
Customer::addBooking
Summary: Creates a new Booking for this customer on a chosen flight, with one PENDING Reservation per supplied passenger name.
Algorithm:
  1. If f is null, listOfPassengerNames is null/empty, or now is null, return false.
  2. If f.openForBooking == false, return false.
  3. If now is not before f.departureTime, return false.
  4. If listOfPassengerNames contains duplicate names (case-insensitive trimmed compare), return false.
  5. Create a new Booking with customer = this.
  6. For each passengerName in listOfPassengerNames:
       - Call newBooking.createReservation(f, passengerName, now); if it returns false, abort and return false.
  7. Append newBooking to this customer's bookings list.
  8. Return true.
Related Features: bookings (List<Booking>)
Related Classes: Flight, Booking, Reservation, Passenger, ReservationStatus
Input:
  - f : Flight — the open flight the customer is booking.
  - now : EDate — current date/time.
  - listOfPassengerNames : List<String> — non-empty list of distinct passenger names.
Output: boolean — true if the booking (and all its reservations) is created, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: f is open for booking, now < f.departureTime, listOfPassengerNames is non-empty and contains no duplicates.
Post-condition: A new Booking is added to this customer's bookings list; one PENDING Reservation per passenger name exists on flight f and is linked to the new booking.

Customer::cancel
Summary: Cancels an existing reservation owned by this customer, provided the flight is still open and has not departed.
Algorithm:
  1. If reservationID is null/empty or now is null, return false.
  2. Search this customer's bookings for a Booking containing a Reservation whose id equals reservationID; if none, return false.
  3. Let r be the matching reservation; let f be r.flight.
  4. If f.openForBooking == false, return false.
  5. If now is not before f.departureTime, return false.
  6. Set r.status = CANCELED.
  7. Return true.
Related Features: bookings (List<Booking>)
Related Classes: Booking, Reservation, Flight, ReservationStatus
Input:
  - reservationID : String — unique id of the reservation to cancel.
  - now : EDate — current date/time.
Output: boolean — true on successful cancellation, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: A reservation with the given id exists within one of this customer's bookings; its flight is still open for booking; current time is before the flight's departureTime; reservation status is not already CANCELED.
Post-condition: The reservation's status is CANCELED; the flight's other reservations remain unchanged.

Customer::confirm
Summary: Confirms an existing reservation owned by this customer, provided the flight is still open and has not departed.
Algorithm:
  1. If reservationID is null/empty or now is null, return false.
  2. Search this customer's bookings for a Booking containing a Reservation whose id equals reservationID; if none, return false.
  3. Let r be the matching reservation; let f be r.flight.
  4. If f.openForBooking == false, return false.
  5. If now is not before f.departureTime, return false.
  6. If r.status is already CONFIRMED, return true (idempotent).
  7. If r.status == CANCELED, return false (cannot confirm a canceled reservation).
  8. Set r.status = CONFIRMED.
  9. Return true.
Related Features: bookings (List<Booking>)
Related Classes: Booking, Reservation, Flight, ReservationStatus
Input:
  - reservationID : String — unique id of the reservation to confirm.
  - now : EDate — current date/time.
Output: boolean — true on successful confirmation, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: A reservation with the given id exists within one of this customer's bookings; its flight is still open for booking; current time is before the flight's departureTime; the reservation is currently PENDING.
Post-condition: The reservation's status becomes CONFIRMED; no other reservation is affected.

# Requirement
```text
Airlines offer different flights. Each flight has its unique id. A flight is open for booking and closed by order of the company. A customer can book one or more flights, for different passengers. A reservation concerns a single flight and a single passenger, has a unique id. A reservation can be confirmed or canceled. A flight has a departure airport and an arrival airport. Each airport has its unique id. A flight has a day and time of departure, and a day and time of arrival (e.g. 2024-12-08 13:00:00). A flight may include stopovers in one or more airport(s). A stopover has a departure time and an arrival time. Each airport serves one or more cities.
Functional requirement:
1.Publish a flight for booking. An airline can publish a newly created flight to make it available for customer bookings. The system first confirms that the flight has valid departure and arrival timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, then checks temporal consistency — currentTime < departureTime < arrivalTime — and route integrity — departureAirport ≠ arrivalAirport. A flight may be published only once, and before publishing, the flight's openForBooking status must be false. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
2.Create a booking for passengers on a flight. A customer selects a specific open flight and supplies a list of passengers names. The system checks that there are no duplicate passengers on the flight and the current time is before the flight departure time. It then generates a booking for the customer and creates a reservation with a unique ID for each passenger in that booking. Each reservation is initially placed in "pending" status until the customer confirms it. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
3.Confirm or cancel an existing reservation in a booking. The system checks that the flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking. The customer can then confirm or cancel a reservation by providing its reservation ID. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
4.Close an open flight. An airline can close an existing flight that has not yet departed. The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
5.Retrieve all confirmed reservations for a specific flight. Given a flight object, the system retrieves every confirmed reservation on that flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking. It returns a list of reservations, or empty list if none exist.
```

# Operation Specifications
Booking::createReservation
Summary: Creates a new Reservation for the named passenger on the supplied flight and attaches it to this booking.
Algorithm:
  1. If f is null, passengerName is null/empty, or now is null, return false.
  2. If f.openForBooking == false, return false.
  3. If now is not before f.departureTime, return false.
  4. Generate a new unique reservation id (e.g., UUID).
  5. Create a new Passenger with name = passengerName.
  6. Create a new Reservation with id, status = PENDING, flight = f, passenger = newPassenger.
  7. Append the new reservation to this Booking's reservations list.
  8. Append the new reservation to f.reservations list.
  9. Return true.
Related Features: reservations (List<Reservation>)
Related Classes: Flight, Reservation, Passenger, ReservationStatus
Input:
  - f : Flight — the flight on which the reservation is created; must be open and not yet departed.
  - passenger : String — the passenger's name.
  - now : EDate — current date/time.
Output: boolean — true on successful creation, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: f is non-null, f.openForBooking == true, now < f.departureTime, passenger is a non-empty string.
Post-condition: A new Reservation with status PENDING exists in both this Booking's reservations and f.reservations; the reservation is associated with a Passenger carrying the supplied name.

3. If there is any error, correct the erroneous specification.
4. Follow the specification format. Output changed specifications only; OMIT unchanged specifications.

# Requirement
```text
Airlines offer different flights. Each flight has its unique id. A flight is open for booking and closed by order of the company. A customer can book one or more flights, for different passengers. A reservation concerns a single flight and a single passenger, has a unique id. A reservation can be confirmed or canceled. A flight has a departure airport and an arrival airport. Each airport has its unique id. A flight has a day and time of departure, and a day and time of arrival (e.g. 2024-12-08 13:00:00). A flight may include stopovers in one or more airport(s). A stopover has a departure time and an arrival time. Each airport serves one or more cities.
Functional requirement:
1.Publish a flight for booking. An airline can publish a newly created flight to make it available for customer bookings. The system first confirms that the flight has valid departure and arrival timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, then checks temporal consistency — currentTime < departureTime < arrivalTime — and route integrity — departureAirport ≠ arrivalAirport. A flight may be published only once, and before publishing, the flight's openForBooking status must be false. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
2.Create a booking for passengers on a flight. A customer selects a specific open flight and supplies a list of passengers names. The system checks that there are no duplicate passengers on the flight and the current time is before the flight departure time. It then generates a booking for the customer and creates a reservation with a unique ID for each passenger in that booking. Each reservation is initially placed in "pending" status until the customer confirms it. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
3.Confirm or cancel an existing reservation in a booking. The system checks that the flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking. The customer can then confirm or cancel a reservation by providing its reservation ID. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
4.Close an open flight. An airline can close an existing flight that has not yet departed. The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
5.Retrieve all confirmed reservations for a specific flight. Given a flight object, the system retrieves every confirmed reservation on that flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking. It returns a list of reservations, or empty list if none exist.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title flights Model

class Airline {
    flights : List<Flight>
    addFlight(f : Flight) : void
    closeFlight(flightId : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    publishFlight(f : Flight, now : EDate) : boolean
    removeFlight(f : Flight) : void
    searchFlights(origin : String, dest : String, date : EDate) : List<Flight>
}
class Airport {
    id : String
    servesForCities : List<City>
    addCity(c : City) : void
}
class Booking {
    customer : Customer
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    createReservation(f : Flight, passenger : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class City {
}
class Customer {
    bookings : List<Booking>
    addBooking(f : Flight, now : EDate, listOfPassengerNames : List<String>) : boolean
    cancel(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    confirm(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class Flight {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    id : String
    openForBooking : boolean
    arrivalAirport : Airport
    departureAirport : Airport
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    stopovers : List<Stopover>
    addStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
    getConfirmedReservations() : List<Reservation>
    removeStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class Passenger {
    name : String
}
class Reservation {
    id : String
    status : ReservationStatus
    flight : Flight
    passenger : Passenger
}
class Stopover {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    airport : Airport
}
enum ReservationStatus {
    PENDING
    CONFIRMED
    CANCELED
}

Airline *-- "*" Flight : flights
Airport --> "*" City : servesForCities
Booking --> Customer : customer
Booking *-- "*" Reservation : reservations
Customer --> "*" Booking : bookings
Flight --> Airport : arrivalAirport
Flight --> Airport : departureAirport
Flight --> "*" Reservation : reservations  'opposite: flight
Flight *-- "*" Stopover : stopovers
Reservation --> Flight : flight  'opposite: reservations
Reservation --> Passenger : passenger
Stopover --> Airport : airport
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
Flight::addStopover
Summary: Adds a stopover (with an airport and arrival/departure times) to this flight.
Algorithm:
  1. If stop is null, stop.airport is null, or now is null, return false.
  2. If stop.arrivalTime or stop.departureTime is not in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, return false.
  3. If !(this.departureTime ≤ stop.arrivalTime < stop.departureTime < this.arrivalTime), return false.
  4. If stopovers already contains a stopover at stop.airport, return false.
  5. Append stop to this.stopovers.
  6. Return true.
Related Features: stopovers (List<Stopover>), departureTime, arrivalTime
Related Classes: Stopover, Airport, EDate
Input:
  - stop : Stopover — the stopover to add; carries an airport and arrival/departure times.
  - now : EDate — current date/time (reserved for future temporal checks).
Output: boolean — true on successful addition, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: stop is non-null; stop.arrivalTime and stop.departureTime fall strictly within [this.departureTime, this.arrivalTime) with arrival < departure; the same airport does not already appear in this.stopovers.
Post-condition: stop is present in this Flight's stopovers list.

Flight::removeStopover
Summary: Removes a stopover from this flight's list of stopovers.
Algorithm:
  1. If stop is null, return false.
  2. Search this.stopovers for an element matching stop (by airport and/or identity); if not found, return false.
  3. Remove the matching stopover from this.stopovers.
  4. Return true.
Related Features: stopovers (List<Stopover>)
Related Classes: Stopover
Input:
  - stop : Stopover — the stopover to remove; must be currently present in this.stopovers.
  - now : EDate — current date/time (reserved for any future temporal preconditions).
Output: boolean — true on successful removal, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: stop is non-null and currently present in this Flight's stopovers list.
Post-condition: stop is no longer present in this.stopovers; departureTime, arrivalTime, and reservations remain unchanged.

Flight::getConfirmedReservations
Summary: Returns every reservation on this flight whose status is CONFIRMED, provided the flight is currently open for booking.
Algorithm:
  1. If this.openForBooking == false, return an empty list.
  2. Initialize an empty result list.
  3. For each reservation in this.reservations:
       If reservation.status == CONFIRMED, add it to the result list.
  4. Return the result list.
Related Features: reservations (List<Reservation>), openForBooking
Related Classes: Reservation, ReservationStatus
Input: (none).
Output: List<Reservation> — confirmed reservations on this flight, or an empty list if the flight is closed or there are no confirmed reservations; never null.
Pre-condition: None; the method tolerates any state, returning an empty list when the flight is closed.
Post-condition: No internal state is modified; a fresh list (possibly empty) is returned to the caller.

# Operation Specifications
Airline::publishFlight
Summary: Publishes a newly created flight so that it becomes available for customer bookings.
Algorithm:
  1. If f is null, return false.
  2. Validate that f.departureTime and f.arrivalTime are in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format; if not, return false.
  3. If !(now < f.departureTime < f.arrivalTime), return false.
  4. If f.departureAirport == f.arrivalAirport (or same id), return false.
  5. If the flight is already present in flights (i.e., already published once), return false.
  6. If f.openForBooking is true, return false.
  7. Set f.openForBooking = true.
  8. Add f to flights list.
  9. Return true.
Related Features: flights (List<Flight>)
Related Classes: Flight, Airport, EDate
Input:
  - f : Flight — the flight to publish; must carry valid times, distinct airports, and openForBooking == false.
  - now : EDate — current date/time used for temporal consistency.
Output: boolean — true on successful publication, false if any check fails.
Pre-condition: f has departureTime/arrivalTime in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, now < f.departureTime < f.arrivalTime, f.departureAirport ≠ f.arrivalAirport, f has not been published before, and f.openForBooking == false.
Post-condition: f.openForBooking == true and f is in this Airline's flights list.

Airline::addFlight
Summary: Adds a given Flight object to this airline's internal collection of flights.
Algorithm:
  1. If f is null, return immediately.
  2. If flights is null, initialize it.
  3. Append f to the flights list.
  4. (Optionally) set a back-reference from f to this Airline.
Related Features: flights (List<Flight>)
Related Classes: Flight
Input: f : Flight — the flight to be added to this airline; must not be null.
Output: void — no return value.
Pre-condition: f is a non-null Flight instance; f may or may not already be linked to this airline.
Post-condition: f is contained in this.airlines's flights list; no other state is altered.

Airline::closeFlight
Summary: Closes an existing open flight (that has not yet departed) and cancels every confirmed reservation on it.
Algorithm:
  1. Search flights for a flight whose id equals flightId; if not found, return false.
  2. If the flight is already not openForBooking, return false.
  3. If the calendar date of now equals the calendar date of flight.departureTime, return false.
  4. If now is not before flight.departureTime, return false (flight already departed).
  5. Set flight.openForBooking = false.
  6. For each reservation in flight.reservations whose status == CONFIRMED, set its status to CANCELED.
  7. Return true.
Related Features: flights (List<Flight>)
Related Classes: Flight, Reservation, ReservationStatus
Input:
  - flightId : String — unique identifier of the flight to close; must match an existing flight.
  - now : EDate — current date/time used to compare with the flight's departureTime and the calendar-day rule.
Output: boolean — true on successful closure, false if any validation fails.
Pre-condition: A flight with the given id exists in flights; the flight is currently open; current time is before departureTime; current calendar day differs from the flight's departure calendar day.
Post-condition: flight.openForBooking == false; every previously CONFIRMED reservation on that flight has status == CANCELED.

Airline::removeFlight
Summary: Removes a given Flight object from this airline's flights list.
Algorithm:
  1. If f is null, return immediately.
  2. Search flights for an entry whose id equals f.id; if not present, return.
  3. Remove the matching element from flights.
Related Features: flights (List<Flight>)
Related Classes: Flight
Input: f : Flight — the flight to be removed; must currently be present in flights.
Output: void.
Pre-condition: f is non-null and currently stored in this Airline's flights list.
Post-condition: f is no longer present in this Airline's flights list; the Flight object's internal state is unchanged.

Airline::searchFlights
Summary: Returns all open flights of this airline whose route and date match the supplied origin, destination, and departure date.
Algorithm:
  1. Initialize an empty result list.
  2. For each flight in flights:
       a. If flight.openForBooking == true,
       b. AND flight.departureAirport.id equals origin,
       c. AND flight.arrivalAirport.id equals dest,
       d. AND the date portion of flight.departureTime equals the date portion of date,
       e. add the flight to the result list.
  3. Return the result list.
Related Features: flights (List<Flight>)
Related Classes: Flight, Airport, EDate
Input:
  - origin : String — id of the desired departure airport.
  - dest : String — id of the desired arrival airport.
  - date : EDate — desired departure calendar date.
Output: List<Flight> — matching open flights, possibly empty; never null.
Pre-condition: origin and dest are non-null airport ids; date is a valid EDate.
Post-condition: The returned list contains zero or more open flights matching the criteria; no internal state is modified.

# Requirement
```text
Airlines offer different flights. Each flight has its unique id. A flight is open for booking and closed by order of the company. A customer can book one or more flights, for different passengers. A reservation concerns a single flight and a single passenger, has a unique id. A reservation can be confirmed or canceled. A flight has a departure airport and an arrival airport. Each airport has its unique id. A flight has a day and time of departure, and a day and time of arrival (e.g. 2024-12-08 13:00:00). A flight may include stopovers in one or more airport(s). A stopover has a departure time and an arrival time. Each airport serves one or more cities.
Functional requirement:
1.Publish a flight for booking. An airline can publish a newly created flight to make it available for customer bookings. The system first confirms that the flight has valid departure and arrival timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, then checks temporal consistency — currentTime < departureTime < arrivalTime — and route integrity — departureAirport ≠ arrivalAirport. A flight may be published only once, and before publishing, the flight's openForBooking status must be false. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
2.Create a booking for passengers on a flight. A customer selects a specific open flight and supplies a list of passengers names. The system checks that there are no duplicate passengers on the flight and the current time is before the flight departure time. It then generates a booking for the customer and creates a reservation with a unique ID for each passenger in that booking. Each reservation is initially placed in "pending" status until the customer confirms it. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
3.Confirm or cancel an existing reservation in a booking. The system checks that the flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking. The customer can then confirm or cancel a reservation by providing its reservation ID. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
4.Close an open flight. An airline can close an existing flight that has not yet departed. The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
5.Retrieve all confirmed reservations for a specific flight. Given a flight object, the system retrieves every confirmed reservation on that flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking. It returns a list of reservations, or empty list if none exist.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title flights Model

class Airline {
    flights : List<Flight>
    addFlight(f : Flight) : void
    closeFlight(flightId : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    publishFlight(f : Flight, now : EDate) : boolean
    removeFlight(f : Flight) : void
    searchFlights(origin : String, dest : String, date : EDate) : List<Flight>
}
class Airport {
    id : String
    servesForCities : List<City>
    addCity(c : City) : void
}
class Booking {
    customer : Customer
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    createReservation(f : Flight, passenger : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class City {
}
class Customer {
    bookings : List<Booking>
    addBooking(f : Flight, now : EDate, listOfPassengerNames : List<String>) : boolean
    cancel(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    confirm(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class Flight {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    id : String
    openForBooking : boolean
    arrivalAirport : Airport
    departureAirport : Airport
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    stopovers : List<Stopover>
    addStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
    getConfirmedReservations() : List<Reservation>
    removeStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class Passenger {
    name : String
}
class Reservation {
    id : String
    status : ReservationStatus
    flight : Flight
    passenger : Passenger
}
class Stopover {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    airport : Airport
}
enum ReservationStatus {
    PENDING
    CONFIRMED
    CANCELED
}

Airline *-- "*" Flight : flights
Airport --> "*" City : servesForCities
Booking --> Customer : customer
Booking *-- "*" Reservation : reservations
Customer --> "*" Booking : bookings
Flight --> Airport : arrivalAirport
Flight --> Airport : departureAirport
Flight --> "*" Reservation : reservations  'opposite: flight
Flight *-- "*" Stopover : stopovers
Reservation --> Flight : flight  'opposite: reservations
Reservation --> Passenger : passenger
Stopover --> Airport : airport
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
Airline::publishFlight
Summary: Publishes a newly created flight so that it becomes available for customer bookings.
Algorithm:
  1. If f is null, return false.
  2. Validate that f.departureTime and f.arrivalTime are in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format; if not, return false.
  3. If !(now < f.departureTime < f.arrivalTime), return false.
  4. If f.departureAirport == f.arrivalAirport (or same id), return false.
  5. If the flight is already present in flights (i.e., already published once), return false.
  6. If f.openForBooking is true, return false.
  7. Set f.openForBooking = true.
  8. Add f to flights list.
  9. Return true.
Related Features: flights (List<Flight>)
Related Classes: Flight, Airport, EDate
Input:
  - f : Flight — the flight to publish; must carry valid times, distinct airports, and openForBooking == false.
  - now : EDate — current date/time used for temporal consistency.
Output: boolean — true on successful publication, false if any check fails.
Pre-condition: f has departureTime/arrivalTime in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, now < f.departureTime < f.arrivalTime, f.departureAirport ≠ f.arrivalAirport, f has not been published before, and f.openForBooking == false.
Post-condition: f.openForBooking == true and f is in this Airline's flights list.

Airline::addFlight
Summary: Adds a given Flight object to this airline's internal collection of flights.
Algorithm:
  1. If f is null, return immediately.
  2. If flights is null, initialize it.
  3. Append f to the flights list.
  4. (Optionally) set a back-reference from f to this Airline.
Related Features: flights (List<Flight>)
Related Classes: Flight
Input: f : Flight — the flight to be added to this airline; must not be null.
Output: void — no return value.
Pre-condition: f is a non-null Flight instance; f may or may not already be linked to this airline.
Post-condition: f is contained in this.airlines's flights list; no other state is altered.

Airline::closeFlight
Summary: Closes an existing open flight (that has not yet departed) and cancels every confirmed reservation on it.
Algorithm:
  1. Search flights for a flight whose id equals flightId; if not found, return false.
  2. If the flight is already not openForBooking, return false.
  3. If the calendar date of now equals the calendar date of flight.departureTime, return false.
  4. If now is not before flight.departureTime, return false (flight already departed).
  5. Set flight.openForBooking = false.
  6. For each reservation in flight.reservations whose status == CONFIRMED, set its status to CANCELED.
  7. Return true.
Related Features: flights (List<Flight>)
Related Classes: Flight, Reservation, ReservationStatus
Input:
  - flightId : String — unique identifier of the flight to close; must match an existing flight.
  - now : EDate — current date/time used to compare with the flight's departureTime and the calendar-day rule.
Output: boolean — true on successful closure, false if any validation fails.
Pre-condition: A flight with the given id exists in flights; the flight is currently open; current time is before departureTime; current calendar day differs from the flight's departure calendar day.
Post-condition: flight.openForBooking == false; every previously CONFIRMED reservation on that flight has status == CANCELED.

Airline::removeFlight
Summary: Removes a given Flight object from this airline's flights list.
Algorithm:
  1. If f is null, return immediately.
  2. Search flights for an entry whose id equals f.id; if not present, return.
  3. Remove the matching element from flights.
Related Features: flights (List<Flight>)
Related Classes: Flight
Input: f : Flight — the flight to be removed; must currently be present in flights.
Output: void.
Pre-condition: f is non-null and currently stored in this Airline's flights list.
Post-condition: f is no longer present in this Airline's flights list; the Flight object's internal state is unchanged.

Airline::searchFlights
Summary: Returns all open flights of this airline whose route and date match the supplied origin, destination, and departure date.
Algorithm:
  1. Initialize an empty result list.
  2. For each flight in flights:
       a. If flight.openForBooking == true,
       b. AND flight.departureAirport.id equals origin,
       c. AND flight.arrivalAirport.id equals dest,
       d. AND the date portion of flight.departureTime equals the date portion of date,
       e. add the flight to the result list.
  3. Return the result list.
Related Features: flights (List<Flight>)
Related Classes: Flight, Airport, EDate
Input:
  - origin : String — id of the desired departure airport.
  - dest : String — id of the desired arrival airport.
  - date : EDate — desired departure calendar date.
Output: List<Flight> — matching open flights, possibly empty; never null.
Pre-condition: origin and dest are non-null airport ids; date is a valid EDate.
Post-condition: The returned list contains zero or more open flights matching the criteria; no internal state is modified.

public void addCity(final City c)
{
  if (c == null)
  {
    return;
  }
  if (servesForCities == null)
  {
    servesForCities = new BasicEList<City>();
  }
  // Check for identity (same EObject URI or same proxy)
  if (c.eIsProxy())
  {
    for (City existing : servesForCities)
    {
      if (existing.eIsProxy() && ((InternalEObject)existing).eProxyURI().equals(((InternalEObject)c).eProxyURI()))
      {
        return;
      }
    }
  }
  else
  {
    for (City existing : servesForCities)
    {
      if (existing == c || EcoreUtil.equals(existing, c))
      {
        return;
      }
    }
  }
  servesForCities.add(c);
}
public void addCity(final City c)
{
  if (c == null)
  {
    return;
  }
  if (servesForCities == null)
  {
    servesForCities = new BasicEList<City>();
  }
  URI cURI = EcoreUtil.getURI(c);
  for (City existing : servesForCities)
  {
    if (existing == c || EcoreUtil.getURI(existing).equals(cURI))
    {
      return;
    }
  }
  servesForCities.add(c);
}

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

```java
   * Summary: Adds a stopover (with an airport and arrival/departure times) to this flight.
   * Algorithm:
```

   *   2. If stop.arrivalTime or stop.departureTime is not in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, return false.
   *   3. If !(this.departureTime ≤ stop.arrivalTime < stop.departureTime < this.arrivalTime), return false.
   *   4. If stopovers already contains a stopover at stop.airport, return false.
   *   5. Append stop to this.stopovers.
   *   6. Return true.
   * Related Features: stopovers (List<Stopover>), departureTime, arrivalTime
   * Related Classes: Stopover, Airport, EDate
   * Input:
   *   - stop : Stopover — the stopover to add; carries an airport and arrival/departure times.
   *   - now : EDate — current date/time (reserved for future temporal checks).
   * Output: boolean — true on successful addition, false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition: stop is non-null; this.departureTime ≤ stop.arrivalTime < stop.departureTime < this.arrivalTime; the same airport does not already appear in this.stopovers.
   * Post-condition: stop is present in this Flight's stopovers list.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean addStopover(final Stopover stop, final Date now)
  {
    // Step 1: null checks
    if (stop == null || stop.getAirport() == null || now == null)
    {
      return false;
    }

    // Step 2: validate stop.arrivalTime and stop.departureTime are in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format
    Date stopArrival = stop.getArrivalTime();
    Date stopDeparture = stop.getDepartureTime();
    if (stopArrival == null || stopDeparture == null)
    {
      return false;
    }
    SimpleDateFormat sdf = new SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss");
    sdf.setLenient(false);
    try
    {
      String arrStr = sdf.format(stopArrival);
      String depStr = sdf.format(stopDeparture);
      Date parsedArr = sdf.parse(arrStr);
      Date parsedDep = sdf.parse(depStr);
      if (!parsedArr.equals(stopArrival) || !parsedDep.equals(stopDeparture))
      {
        return false;
      }
    }
    catch (ParseException ex)
    {
      return false;
    }

    // Step 3: temporal ordering check
    Date flightDeparture = this.getDepartureTime();
    Date flightArrival = this.getArrivalTime();
    if (flightDeparture == null || flightArrival == null)
    {
      return false;
    }
    // this.departureTime ≤ stop.arrivalTime
    if (flightDeparture.after(stopArrival))
    {
      return false;
    }
    // stop.arrivalTime < stop.departureTime
    if (!stopArrival.before(stopDeparture))
    {
      return false;
    }
    // stop.departureTime < this.arrivalTime
    if (!stopDeparture.before(flightArrival))
    {
      return false;
    }

    // Step 4: ensure no existing stopover at the same airport
    Airport targetAirport = stop.getAirport();
    for (Stopover existing : this.getStopovers())
    {
      if (existing != null && existing.getAirport() != null && existing.getAirport().equals(targetAirport))
      {
        return false;
      }
    }

    // Step 5: append stop to this.stopovers
    this.getStopovers().add(stop);

    // Step 6: success
    return true;
  }

    if (stop == null)
    {
      return false;

    // Step 2: search for a matching stopover
    EList<Stopover> currentStopovers = this.getStopovers();
    Stopover match = null;
    Airport stopAirport = stop.getAirport();
    for (Stopover existing : currentStopovers)
    {
      if (existing == null)
      {
        continue;
      }
      if (existing == stop)
      {
        match = existing;
        break;
      }
      if (stopAirport != null)
      {
        Airport existingAirport = existing.getAirport();
        if (existingAirport != null && existingAirport.equals(stopAirport))
        {

          break;
        }
      }
    }
    if (match == null)
    {
      return false;
    }

    // Step 3: remove the matching stopover
    currentStopovers.remove(match);

    return true;

      }
    }

    // Step 4: return the result list
    return result;
  }

   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Creates a new Reservation for the named passenger on the supplied flight and attaches it to this booking.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If f is null, passengerName is null/empty, or now is null, return false.
   *   2. If f.openForBooking == false, return false.
   *   3. If now is not before f.departureTime, return false.
   *   4. Generate a new unique reservation id (e.g., UUID).
   *   5. Create a new Passenger with name = passengerName.
   *   6. Create a new Reservation with id, status = PENDING, flight = f, passenger = newPassenger.
   *   7. Append the new reservation to this Booking's reservations list.
   *   8. Append the new reservation to f.reservations list.
   *   9. Return true.
   * Related Features: reservations (List<Reservation>)
   * Related Classes: Flight, Reservation, Passenger, ReservationStatus
   * Input:
   *   - f : Flight — the flight on which the reservation is created; must be open and not yet departed.
   *   - passenger : String — the passenger's name.
   *   - now : EDate — current date/time.
   * Output: boolean — true on successful creation, false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition: f is non-null, f.openForBooking == true, now < f.departureTime, passenger is a non-empty string.
   * Post-condition: A new Reservation with status PENDING exists in both this Booking's reservations and f.reservations; the reservation is associated with a Passenger carrying the supplied name.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean createReservation(final Flight f, final String passenger, final Date now)
  {
    // Step 1: Validate inputs
    if (f == null || passenger == null || passenger.isEmpty() || now == null)
    {
      return false;
    }

    // Step 2: Check if the flight is open for booking
    if (!f.isOpenForBooking())
    {
      return false;
    }

    // Step 3: Check that now is before the flight's departure time
    Date departureTime = f.getDepartureTime();
    if (departureTime == null || !now.before(departureTime))
    {
      return false;
    }

    // Step 4: Generate a new unique reservation id
    String reservationId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();

    // Step 5: Create a new Passenger with the supplied name
    Passenger newPassenger = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createPassenger();
    newPassenger.setName(passenger);

    // Step 6: Create a new Reservation with status PENDING
    Reservation newReservation = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createReservation();
    newReservation.setId(reservationId);
    newReservation.setStatus(ReservationStatus.PENDING);
    newReservation.setPassenger(newPassenger);
    newReservation.setFlight(f);

    // Step 7: Append the new reservation to this Booking's reservations list
    this.getReservations().add(newReservation);

    // Step 8: Append the new reservation to f.reservations list
    f.getReservations().add(newReservation);

    // Step 9: Return true on successful creation
    return true;
  }

```java
   * Summary: Creates a new Booking for this customer on a chosen flight, with one PENDING Reservation per supplied passenger name, provided no passenger would be duplicated on the flight.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If f is null, listOfPassengerNames is null/empty, or now is null, return false.
   *   2. If f.openForBooking == false, return false.
   *   3. If now is not before f.departureTime, return false.
   *   4. If listOfPassengerNames contains duplicate names (case-insensitive trimmed compare), return false.
   *   5. For each passengerName in listOfPassengerNames, check if any existing reservation in f.reservations has a passenger whose name matches passengerName (case-insensitive trimmed compare); if so, return false.

   *        - Call newBooking.createReservation(f, passengerName, now); if it returns false, abort and return false.
   *   8. Append newBooking to this customer's bookings list.
   *   9. Return true.
   * Related Features: bookings (List<Booking>)
   * Related Classes: Flight, Booking, Reservation, Passenger, ReservationStatus

   * Pre-condition: f is open for booking, now < f.departureTime, listOfPassengerNames is non-empty and contains no duplicates.
   * Post-condition: A new Booking is added to this customer's bookings list; one PENDING Reservation per passenger name exists on flight f and is linked to the new booking; no passenger appears more than once on flight f.
```

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

    // Step 1: Validate inputs.
    if (f == null || now == null || listOfPassengerNames == null || listOfPassengerNames.isEmpty())
    {
      return false;
    }
    // Step 2: Flight must be open for booking.
    if (!f.isOpenForBooking())
    {
      return false;
    }
    // Step 3: Now must be before flight departure time.
    if (!now.before(f.getDepartureTime()))
    {
      return false;
    }
    // Step 4: No duplicate names in the supplied list (case-insensitive, trimmed).
    for (int i = 0; i < listOfPassengerNames.size(); i++)

      String nameI = listOfPassengerNames.get(i);

      }

          return false;
        }
      }
    }
    // Step 5: Ensure no supplied passenger name already exists on the flight.
    EList<Reservation> existingReservations = f.getReservations();
    for (String passengerName : listOfPassengerNames)
    {
      String trimmedName = passengerName.trim();
      for (Reservation existing : existingReservations)
      {
        Passenger p = existing.getPassenger();
        if (p != null && p.getName() != null && p.getName().trim().equalsIgnoreCase(trimmedName))
        {
          return false;
        }

        return false;

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the context.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

   *   - now : EDate — current date/time used for temporal consistency.
   * Output: boolean — true on successful publication, false if any check fails.
   * Pre-condition: f has departureTime/arrivalTime in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, now < f.departureTime < f.arrivalTime, f.departureAirport ≠ f.arrivalAirport, f has not been published before, and f.openForBooking == false.
   * Post-condition: f.openForBooking == true and f is in this Airline's flights list.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean publishFlight(final Flight f, final Date now)
  {
    if (f == null)
    {
      return false;
    }
    final Date departureTime = f.getDepartureTime();
    final Date arrivalTime = f.getArrivalTime();
    final SimpleDateFormat sdf = new SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss");
    sdf.setLenient(false);
    try
    {
      final Date parsedDeparture = sdf.parse(sdf.format(departureTime));
      if (parsedDeparture == null)
      {
        return false;
      }
      final Date parsedArrival = sdf.parse(sdf.format(arrivalTime));
      if (parsedArrival == null)
      {
        return false;
      }
    }
    catch (ParseException e)
    {
      return false;
    }
    if (!(now.before(departureTime) && departureTime.before(arrivalTime)))
    {
      return false;
    }
    final Airport dep = f.getDepartureAirport();
    final Airport arr = f.getArrivalAirport();
    if (dep == null || arr == null)
    {
      return false;
    }
    if (dep == arr)
    {
      return false;
    }
    if (dep.getId() != null && dep.getId().equals(arr.getId()))
    {
      return false;
    }
    final EList<Flight> current = getFlights();
    for (Flight existing : current)
    {
      if (existing == f || (existing != null && f.getId() != null && f.getId().equals(existing.getId())))
      {
        return false;
      }
    }
    if (f.isOpenForBooking())
    {
      return false;
    }
    f.setOpenForBooking(true);
    current.add(f);
    return true;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Closes an existing open flight (that has not yet departed) and cancels every confirmed reservation on it.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Search flights for a flight whose id equals flightId; if not found, return false.
   *   2. If the flight is already not openForBooking, return false.
   *   3. If the calendar date of now equals the calendar date of flight.departureTime, return false.
   *   4. If now is not before flight.departureTime, return false (flight already departed).
   *   5. Set flight.openForBooking = false.
   *   6. For each reservation in flight.reservations whose status == CONFIRMED, set its status to CANCELED.
   *   7. Return true.
   * Related Features: flights (List<Flight>)
   * Related Classes: Flight, Reservation, ReservationStatus
   * Input:
   *   - flightId : String — unique identifier of the flight to close; must match an existing flight.
   *   - now : EDate — current date/time used to compare with the flight's departureTime and the calendar-day rule.
   * Output: boolean — true on successful closure, false if any validation fails.
   * Pre-condition: A flight with the given id exists in flights; the flight is currently open; current time is before departureTime; current calendar day differs from the flight's departure calendar day.
   * Post-condition: flight.openForBooking == false; every previously CONFIRMED reservation on that flight has status == CANCELED.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean closeFlight(final String flightId, final Date now)
  {
    Flight target = null;
    for (Flight flight : getFlights())
    {
      if (flight != null && flightId != null && flightId.equals(flight.getId()))
      {
        target = flight;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (target == null)
    {
      return false;
    }
    if (!target.isOpenForBooking())
    {
      return false;
    }
    final Date departureTime = target.getDepartureTime();
    final Calendar nowCal = Calendar.getInstance();
    nowCal.setTime(now);
    final Calendar depCal = Calendar.getInstance();
    depCal.setTime(departureTime);
    if (nowCal.get(Calendar.YEAR) == depCal.get(Calendar.YEAR) &&
        nowCal.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR) == depCal.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR))
    {
      return false;
    }
    if (!now.before(departureTime))
    {
      return false;
    }
    target.setOpenForBooking(false);
    for (Reservation reservation : target.getReservations())
    {
      if (reservation != null && reservation.getStatus() == ReservationStatus.CONFIRMED)
      {
        reservation.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CANCELED);
      }
    }
    return true;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Returns all open flights of this airline whose route and date match the supplied origin, destination, and departure date.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Initialize an empty result list.
   *   2. For each flight in flights:
   *        a. If flight.openForBooking == true,
   *        b. AND flight.departureAirport.id equals origin,
   *        c. AND flight.arrivalAirport.id equals dest,
   *        d. AND the date portion of flight.departureTime equals the date portion of date,
   *        e. add the flight to the result list.
   *   3. Return the result list.
   * Related Features: flights (List<Flight>)
   * Related Classes: Flight, Airport, EDate
   * Input:
   *   - origin : String — id of the desired departure airport.
   *   - dest : String — id of the desired arrival airport.
   *   - date : EDate — desired departure calendar date.
   * Output: List<Flight> — matching open flights, possibly empty; never null.
   * Pre-condition: origin and dest are non-null airport ids; date is a valid EDate.
   * Post-condition: The returned list contains zero or more open flights matching the criteria; no internal state is modified.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public EList<Flight> searchFlights(final String origin, final String dest, final Date date)
  {
    final EList<Flight> result = new BasicEList<Flight>();
    final Calendar targetCal = Calendar.getInstance();
    targetCal.setTime(date);
    final int targetYear = targetCal.get(Calendar.YEAR);
    final int targetDay = targetCal.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR);
    for (Flight flight : getFlights())
    {
      if (flight == null || !flight.isOpenForBooking())
      {
        continue;
      }
      final Airport dep = flight.getDepartureAirport();
      final Airport arr = flight.getArrivalAirport();
      if (dep == null || arr == null)
      {
        continue;
      }
      if (origin != null && !origin.equals(dep.getId()))
      {
        continue;
      }
      if (dest != null && !dest.equals(arr.getId()))
      {
        continue;
      }
      final Date departureTime = flight.getDepartureTime();
      if (departureTime == null)
      {
        continue;
      }
      final Calendar flightCal = Calendar.getInstance();
      flightCal.setTime(departureTime);
      if (flightCal.get(Calendar.YEAR) != targetYear || flightCal.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR) != targetDay)
      {
        continue;
      }
      result.add(flight);
    }
    return result;
  }
